5a:T1ccac,

import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="sap_s4hana" color="#FFFFFF" />

使用说明 [#使用说明]

Connect SAP S4HANA Cloud Public Edition with per-tenant OAuth 2.0 client credentials configured in your Communication Arrangements. Read and create business partners, customers, suppliers, sales orders, deliveries (inbound/outbound), billing documents, products, stock and material documents, purchase requisitions, purchase orders, and supplier invoices, or run arbitrary OData v2 queries against any whitelisted Communication Scenario.

工具 [#工具]

sap_s4hana_list_business_partners [#sap_s4hana_list_business_partners]

List business partners from SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_BUSINESS\_PARTNER, A\_BusinessPartner) with optional OData $filter, $top, $skip, $orderby, $select, $expand.

输入 [#输入]

| 参数               | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                     |
| ---------------- | ------ | -- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`      | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host) |
| `region`         | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                           |
| `clientId`       | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                             |
| `clientSecret`   | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                         |
| `deploymentType` | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise               |

| `tokenUrl`       | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                   |
| `username`       | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                           |
| `password`       | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                           |
| `filter`         | string | 否  | OData $filter expression (e.g., "BusinessPartnerCategory eq '1'")                      |
| `top`            | number | 否  | Maximum results to return ($top)                                                       |
| `skip`           | number | 否  | Number of results to skip ($skip)                                                      |

sap_s4hana_create_business_partner [#sap_s4hana_create_business_partner]

Create a business partner in SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_BUSINESS\_PARTNER, A\_BusinessPartner). For Person category 1 provide FirstName and LastName. For Organization category 2 provide OrganizationBPName1.

输入 [#输入-2]

| 参数                        | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                        |
| ------------------------- | ------ | -- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`               | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host)    |
| `region`                  | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                              |
| `clientId`                | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                |
| `clientSecret`            | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                            |
| `deploymentType`          | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise                  |
| `authType`                | string | 否  | Authentication type: oauth\_client\_credentials (default) or basic                        |
| `baseUrl`                 | string | 否  | Base URL of the S/4HANA host (Cloud Private / On-Premise)                                 |
| `tokenUrl`                | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                      |
| `username`                | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                              |
| `password`                | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                              |
| `businessPartnerCategory` | string | 是  | BusinessPartnerCategory: "1" Person, "2" Organization, "3" Group                          |
| `businessPartnerGrouping` | string | 是  | BusinessPartnerGrouping (number range / role grouping configured in S/4HANA, e.g. "0001") |
| `firstName`               | string | 否  | FirstName (required for Person)                                                           |
| `lastName`                | string | 否  | LastName (required for Person)                                                            |
| `organizationBPName1`     | string | 否  | OrganizationBPName1 (required for Organization)                                           |
| `body`                    | json   | 否  | Optional additional A\_BusinessPartner fields merged into the create payload              |

输出 [#输出-2]

| 参数       | 类型     | 描述                                |
| -------- | ------ | --------------------------------- |
| `status` | number | HTTP status code returned by SAP  |
| `data`   | json   | Created A\_BusinessPartner entity |

Update fields on an A\_BusinessPartner entity in SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_BUSINESS\_PARTNER). PATCH only sends the fields you provide; existing values are preserved. If-Match defaults to a wildcard (unconditional) — for safe concurrent updates pass the ETag from a prior GET to avoid lost updates.

Create a sales order in SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_SALES\_ORDER\_SRV, A\_SalesOrder) with deep insert of sales order items via to\_Item.

输入 [#输入-12]

| 参数                     | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------- | ------ | -- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`            | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host)                                                                                |
| `region`               | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                                                                                                          |
| `clientId`             | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                                                            |
| `clientSecret`         | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                                                        |
| `deploymentType`       | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise                                                                                              |
| `authType`             | string | 否  | Authentication type: oauth\_client\_credentials (default) or basic                                                                                                    |
| `baseUrl`              | string | 否  | Base URL of the S/4HANA host (Cloud Private / On-Premise)                                                                                                             |
| `tokenUrl`             | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                                                                                                  |
| `username`             | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                                                          |
| `password`             | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                                                          |
| `salesOrderType`       | string | 是  | SalesOrderType (e.g., "OR" Standard Order)                                                                                                                            |
| `salesOrganization`    | string | 是  | SalesOrganization (4 chars, e.g., "1010")                                                                                                                             |
| `distributionChannel`  | string | 是  | DistributionChannel (2 chars, e.g., "10")                                                                                                                             |
| `organizationDivision` | string | 是  | OrganizationDivision (2 chars, e.g., "00")                                                                                                                            |
| `soldToParty`          | string | 是  | SoldToParty business partner key (up to 10 chars)                                                                                                                     |
| `items`                | json   | 是  | Array of sales order items for to\_Item deep insert. Each item should include Material and RequestedQuantity (e.g., \[\{"Material":"TG11","RequestedQuantity":"1"}]). |
| `body`                 | json   | 否  | Optional additional A\_SalesOrder fields merged into the create payload                                                                                               |

输出 [#输出-12]

| 参数       | 类型     | 描述                                                                         |
| -------- | ------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `status` | number | HTTP status code returned by SAP                                           |
| `data`   | json   | Created A\_SalesOrder entity (with deep-inserted items if expanded by SAP) |

sap_s4hana_update_sales_order [#sap_s4hana_update_sales_order]

Update fields on an A\_SalesOrder entity in SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_SALES\_ORDER\_SRV). PATCH only sends the fields you provide; existing values are preserved. If-Match defaults to a wildcard (unconditional) — for safe concurrent updates pass the ETag from a prior GET to avoid lost updates.

输入 [#输入-13]

| 参数               | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------- | ------ | -- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`      | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host)                                          |
| `region`         | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                                                                    |
| `clientId`       | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                      |
| `clientSecret`   | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                  |
| `deploymentType` | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise                                                        |
| `authType`       | string | 否  | Authentication type: oauth\_client\_credentials (default) or basic                                                              |
| `baseUrl`        | string | 否  | Base URL of the S/4HANA host (Cloud Private / On-Premise)                                                                       |
| `tokenUrl`       | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                                                            |
| `username`       | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                    |
| `password`       | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                    |
| `salesOrder`     | string | 是  | SalesOrder key to update (string, up to 10 characters)                                                                          |
| `body`           | json   | 是  | JSON object with A\_SalesOrder fields to update (e.g., \{"PurchaseOrderByCustomer":"PO-12345","HeaderBillingBlockReason":"01"}) |
| `ifMatch`        | string | 否  | If-Match ETag for optimistic concurrency. Defaults to "\*" (unconditional).                                                     |

输出 [#输出-13]

| 参数       | 类型     | 描述                                                                      |
| -------- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `status` | number | HTTP status code returned by SAP (204 on success)                       |
| `data`   | json   | Null on 204 success, or updated A\_SalesOrder entity if SAP returns one |

sap_s4hana_update_product [#sap_s4hana_update_product]

Update fields on an A\_Product entity in SAP S/4HANA Cloud (API\_PRODUCT\_SRV). PATCH only sends the fields you provide; existing values are preserved. Flat scalar header fields only — deep/multi-entity updates across navigation properties are not supported by API\_PRODUCT\_SRV PATCH/PUT (see SAP KBA 2833338); update child entities (plant, valuation, sales data, etc.) via their own endpoints. If-Match defaults to a wildcard (unconditional) — for safe concurrent updates pass the ETag from a prior GET.

输入 [#输入-23]

| 参数               | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                        |
| ---------------- | ------ | -- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`      | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host)                    |
| `region`         | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                                              |
| `clientId`       | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                |
| `clientSecret`   | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                            |
| `deploymentType` | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise                                  |
| `authType`       | string | 否  | Authentication type: oauth\_client\_credentials (default) or basic                                        |
| `baseUrl`        | string | 否  | Base URL of the S/4HANA host (Cloud Private / On-Premise)                                                 |
| `tokenUrl`       | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                                      |

PurchaseRequisition is auto-assigned by SAP from the document number range; provide line items via the to\_PurchaseReqnItem deep-insert array. Note: API\_PURCHASEREQ\_PROCESS\_SRV is deprecated since S/4HANA Cloud Public Edition 2402; the successor is API\_PURCHASEREQUISITION\_2 (OData v4). This tool still works against tenants where the legacy service is enabled.

| `region`                  | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `clientId`                | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                                                                                          |
| `clientSecret`            | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                                                                                      |

| `username`                | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `password`                | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `purchaseRequisitionType` | string | 是  | PurchaseRequisitionType (e.g., "NB" Standard PR)                                                                                                                                                    |
| `items`                   | json   | 是  | to\_PurchaseReqnItem deep-insert array (e.g., \[\{"PurchaseRequisitionItem":"10","Material":"TG11","RequestedQuantity":"5","Plant":"1010","BaseUnit":"PC","DeliveryDate":"/Date(1735689600000)/"}]) |
| `body`                    | json   | 否  | Additional A\_PurchaseRequisitionHeader fields merged into the create payload (e.g., \{"PurchaseRequisitionDescription":"Office supplies"})                                                         |

| 参数               | 类型     | 必填 | 描述                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------- | ------ | -- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `subdomain`      | string | 是  | SAP BTP subaccount subdomain (technical name of your subaccount, not the S/4HANA host)                                            |
| `region`         | string | 是  | BTP region (e.g. eu10, us10)                                                                                                      |
| `clientId`       | string | 是  | OAuth client ID from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                        |
| `clientSecret`   | string | 是  | OAuth client secret from the S/4HANA Communication Arrangement                                                                    |
| `deploymentType` | string | 否  | Deployment type: cloud\_public (default), cloud\_private, or on\_premise                                                          |
| `authType`       | string | 否  | Authentication type: oauth\_client\_credentials (default) or basic                                                                |
| `baseUrl`        | string | 否  | Base URL of the S/4HANA host (Cloud Private / On-Premise)                                                                         |
| `tokenUrl`       | string | 否  | OAuth token URL (Cloud Private / On-Premise + OAuth)                                                                              |
| `username`       | string | 否  | Username for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                      |
| `password`       | string | 否  | Password for HTTP Basic auth                                                                                                      |
| `purchaseOrder`  | string | 是  | PurchaseOrder key to update (string, up to 10 characters)                                                                         |
| `body`           | json   | 是  | JSON object with A\_PurchaseOrder fields to update (e.g., \{"PurchasingGroup":"002","PurchaseOrderDate":"/Date(1735689600000)/"}) |
| `ifMatch`        | string | 否  | If-Match ETag for optimistic concurrency. Defaults to "\*" (unconditional).                                                       |

输出 [#输出-34]

| 参数       | 类型     | 描述                                                                         |
| -------- | ------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `status` | number | HTTP status code returned by SAP (204 on success)                          |
| `data`   | json   | Null on 204 success, or updated A\_PurchaseOrder entity if SAP returns one |
